# CDPOS File Lookup & Group Mapping

This document details the disc layout table `CDPOS.DAT` and how files are grouped and loaded by the game engine.

## CDPOS.DAT Architecture

`CDPOS.DAT` (24,976 bytes, 3,122 entries of 8 bytes each) is loaded at boot by `LoadCdPosTablefromCd()` into RAM at `0x800645D0`.

Each 8-byte entry contains:
- `Offset 0..3` (uint32 little-endian): Sector LBA on disc (the game does `LBA - 1` to resolve the ISO9660 sector).
- `Offset 4..7` (uint32 little-endian): File size in bytes (for XA video streams in Group 0, this reflects total XA sector payload size `sectors * 2336`).

Entries `0` and `1` represent disc root files (`SYSTEM.CNF` and `ANOTHER.EXE`). Entries `2` through `3121` map to the 3,120 files in `PROGDATA`.

The executable defines group start indices in the boundary array `D_8004B440` (see `GetFileCountInGroup` and `GetCdFileSectorInfo`).

## Group Overview

| Group | Folder | Count | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| 00 | `assets/jp/progdata/video/` | 66 | Full-Motion Video streams (MDEC STR) |
| 01 | `assets/jp/progdata/system/` | 61 | System UI, Fonts, Chapter cards, Vibration profiles |
| 02 | `assets/jp/progdata/dictionary/` | 40 | Kana-to-Kanji dictionaries |
| 03 | `assets/jp/progdata/dialogue/` | 53 | Dialogue box chrome and text font glyphs |
| 04 | `assets/jp/progdata/menus/` | 239 | System menus, logic overlays, scenario data |
| 05 | `assets/jp/progdata/scripts/` | 343 | Scenario scripts / VM bytecode (ZZS) organized by author |
| 06 | `assets/jp/progdata/backgrounds/` | 1219 | Photographic background images and event stills |
| 07 | `assets/jp/progdata/animations/` | 811 | Character portrait facial animations and expressions |
| 08 | `assets/jp/progdata/audio/` | 287 | SPU sound effects, audio sample banks, and music |
| 09 | `assets/jp/progdata/dummy/` | 1 | Disc padding file |

---
